## Document 018: 2018 年の過去問は使えるか 利上げ打ち止めが株価反転の起点に

Source PDF: all report pdf/2018 年の過去問は使えるか 利上げ打ち止めが株価反転の起点に.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-24
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026年 11月 24日(火)

1/4 
2018年の過去問は使えるか 利上げ打ち止めが株価反転の起点に 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・10月中旬以降のＡＩ・半導体株の下落要因は、ＡＩ投資の減速懸念もさることながら、金利
上昇が効いていると筆者は判断している。３月以降の原油高を受けて、主要中央銀行が利上
げに踏み切り、世界的にイールドカーブ全体が上昇したことで、①株式の債券に対する相対
的な魅力が低下したほか、②金利負担増加がＡＩ関連投資を抑制するとの懸念が生じた。②
は米新興ＡＩ企業の資金調達を巡る懸念にも繋がった。その他、③米中間選挙における民主
党の躍進により生じたＡＩ投資規制の強化懸念、④エヌビディアＣＥＯの辞任（その後復
帰）など特殊な要因も重なった。

・このうち④は事態が急変した。エヌビディア社は11月23日にジェンセン・ファン氏の即時復
帰を発表。これによって、当面、ＡＩ産業における同氏の属人的問題は解消されたと考えて
問題ないだろう。とはいえ、①、②、③に絡んだ問題は残存している。

・このうち③の民主党によるデータセンター投資の規制強化に対する観測は、新体制で議会が
発足する2027年１月以降も蒸し返される可能性があるだろう。全体としてみれば、民主党は
必ずしもデータセンター建設、すなわちＡＩ開発に否定的ではないものの、選挙の焦点の一
つであったデータセンターの建設許可は政治的な人質になり易く、直ちに解決するとは考え
・日経平均株価は先行き 12ヶ月 72,000円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPYは先行き 12ヶ月 155円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を 12月に 1.5％とした後、27年末までに 2.25％とするだろう 
・FEDはFF金利（誘導目標上限）を、12月に4.25％へ引き上げるだろう

### Page 2

2/4 
にくい。データセンターの新設が後ずれするだけでも、ネオクラウドなど新興ＡＩ各社の資
金繰り懸念は高まってしまう。財務体質の強いハイパースケーラーにとってもＡＩ投資のキ
ャッシュフロー好転や収益化の遅れは大きな痛手となる。

・他方、①と②はここへ来て最悪期脱出の兆候もみられるようになってきた。最も重要なのは
原油価格の低下であろう。ＷＴＩ原油価格は11月23日に80ドルを割れる場面があった。Ａ
Ｉ・半導体株の逆風となっている世界的な金利上昇について、その背景に原油高があること
は論を俟たない。ここで夏場以降、米実質金利上昇と米国株ＰＥＲ低下が併存してきたこと
に改めて注目したい。ＡＩ・半導体株を巡っては「業績見通しが上向いているのに株価がつ
いてこない、それどころか下落する」と言われてきた。このグラフは、金利上昇がこの疑問
解消にあたって非常に重要な要因であることを如実に物語る。夏場以降、「一時的」とみら
れていた原油高が長期化したことで、Ｆｅｄを中心とする主要中央銀行の利上げ観測が高ま
ることで金利が上昇し、その裏で株式のバリュエーションが低下していったことがわかる。

・Ｆｅｄが一段とタカ派に傾斜するようだと、株式市場は更なる調整に見舞われることにな
る。振り返ると、10月ＣＰＩが発表される直前の11月10日にウォーラー理事が突如として
50bpの利上げに言及したことが、その後の株価急落のきっかけとなったように思える。同時
期にエヌビディアＣＥＯの進退問題が大きく取り沙汰されたことで、ウォーラー理事の発言
はかき消された感があったが、この発言によって大幅利上げに対する警戒感が強まり、株式
市場への打撃となったことは認識しておく必要があろう。他方、ウォーラー理事と同等の影
響力を持つウィリアムズ・ＮＹ連銀総裁は追加利上げの必要性を認めつつも「関税、地政学

3/4 
リスク、ＡＩ投資による値上がりは一部の財に表れているものの、幅広く持続的なインフレ
へ大きく波及している証拠は乏しい」との見解を示しており、現時点で大幅な利上げに距離
を置いている。この点は株式市場にとって安心感がある。同総裁は「来年に２％をやや上回
る水準まで鈍化した後、2028年にＦＲＢが目標とする２％に達する」との予想を示してい
る。

・これらを踏まえると相場の潮目が変化するのは、現時点でタカ派方向へ傾倒しているウォー
ラー理事の見解がハト派方向に変化する際ではないか。もちろん、それはインフレデータ次
第であるが、足もとで原油価格が低下していることに鑑みると、同理事のインフレ警戒感は
今後徐々に弱まっていっても不思議ではない。またＡＩ・半導体株の急落によって金融環境
が急速かつ過度に引き締まってしまうことを懸念する可能性もある。

・バーナンキ氏の後に続くＦＲＢ議長、すなわちイエレン氏やパウエル氏は株式市場の急落に
際してハト派的な見解を示し、相場反転のきっかけとなり、それは「イエレン・プット」な
どと言われた。そうした市場への口先介入に距離を置くウォーシュ現議長は「ウォーシュ・
プット」という言葉をいかにも嫌いそうであり、株価急落局面でも口数の少ない役を演じ切
りそうである。そうした事情もあり、ウォーラー理事やウィリアムズ総裁の発言が存在感を
増しそうである。

・なお、過去数ヶ月の株価下落は2018年との類似性が指摘される。当時も現在も、景気が底堅
く推移するもとで、Ｆｅｄが利上げを継続して実質金利が上昇し、株価がＰＥＲ低下を伴っ
て下落した。2018年は株価下落にもかかわらず、Ｆｅｄが12月も利上げを継続し、なおかつ
（今よりずっと信頼されていた）ドットチャートが翌年の追加利上げを示唆していた。株式
相場の転換点となったのは、2019年１月４日のパウエル議長の発言。株式市場をなだめる意
図もあってか「政策判断を忍耐強く行い、市場の示すリスクに注意を払い、必要なら政策姿
勢を大きく変更する」と軌道修正を図った。結局、株価は2018年12月24日が大底となり、そ
の後はＦｅｄの利下げ（2019年7・9・10月）を追い風として、コロナ禍に直面するまで
S&P500は10％超の下落を経験することなく上昇した。

・2018年の過去問を今次局面に応用すると、12月ＦＯＭＣにおける利上げが最後になるとの見
方が強まれば、株価はその近辺が底になるのではないか。そうした意味においても上記２名

## Document 047: 20261121【投研報告】市場快訊_輝達會計調查疑慮升溫，全球股市承壓、避險需求攀升

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261121【投研報告】市場快訊_輝達會計調查疑慮升溫，全球股市承壓、避險需求攀升.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (市場評論)
Date: 2026-11-21

輝達會計調查疑慮升溫，全球股市承壓、避險需求攀升 
                                   2026年 11月 21日 
一、 市場動態 
1. 美股延續弱勢，半導體類股跌幅尤為顯著：美國股市 11月 20日開盤後延續弱勢，盤
中主要股價指數震盪走低，尾盤賣壓進一步擴大，其中科技股表現最為疲弱，輝達、
AMD、Broadcom及 Micron等半導體個股同步重挫，賣壓亦擴散至伺服器、網路設
備及其他 AI 基礎建設相關企業。終場道瓊工業指數下跌 1.93%，收 48,147.33 點；
S&P 500 指數下跌 3.70%，收 6,910.39 點；那斯達克綜合指數下跌 5.28%，收
22,617.73點；費城半導體指數重挫 10.73%，收 8,408.59點。產業表現方面，科技
、工業及原物料類股跌幅較大，民生必需消費及醫療保健類股表現相對抗跌。 
2. 全球股市重挫帶動避險需求，美債殖利率全面回落：11月 20日美國公債價格走揚，
10年期公債殖利率單日下跌 10個基點至 4.95%，重新回落至 5%以下。另一方面，
VIX指數明顯攀升至 37附近，顯示市場波動程度進一步升高。整體而言，當日全球金
融市場呈現明顯的股債表現分化，科技及半導體類股跌幅尤為顯著，政府公債則獲得
避險買盤青睞，市場資金配置呈現由風險性資產轉向高品質防禦性資產的跡象。 
表一：11/20美股主要指數與美國公債 10年期利率表現 
指數 收盤點位／殖利率 單日變動 漲跌幅／基點
道瓊工業指數 48,147.33 -949.73點 -1.93%
S&P 500指數 6,910.39 -265.44點 -3.70%
那斯達克綜合指數 22,617.73 -1,261.47點 -5.28%
費城半導體指數 8,408.59 -1,011.13點 -10.73%
美國10年期公債殖利率 4.95% -10 個基點 -
資料來源：Yahoo Finance / Trading View，資料日期：2026/11/20

二、 下跌原因 
1. 輝達 SEC調查方向進一步明朗，企業財務資訊可信度面臨挑戰：11月 17日 WSJ已
披露 SEC正在調查輝達，但當時尚未掌握具體調查方向。11月 20日 Reuters引述兩
名知情人士指出，SEC執法人員正在檢視輝達可能存在的會計問題，為此次調查的重
要新進展。相較於先前市場主要關注管理層異動、公司治理及出口管制，此次消息使
投資人進一步擔憂企業營收認列、財務揭露及交易安排。輝達在消息公布前股價已明
顯走弱，報導發布後跌幅進一步擴大，顯示會計調查消息為加劇輝達股價賣壓的重要
催化因素，亦可能加重市場對 AI產業財務透明度的疑慮。惟目前 SEC尚未正式公布
具體調查範圍，亦未認定輝達存在會計違規。

### Page 2

4. AI投資回報疑慮延續，Anthropic掛牌未能扭轉市場情緒：隨著大型雲端業者持續擴
大資料中心投資，設備折舊、電力及融資成本同步增加，市場開始關注 AI服務收入能

否支應相關支出。Anthropic在上市首日上漲 17.1%後，次日股價下跌 9.1%，亦引發
資金排擠及獲利了結疑慮，反映市場對 AI產業的評價標準已由營收成長轉向投資回報
及自由現金流，對 AI相關資產的評價逐漸轉趨審慎。
5. 科技股持倉集中與去槓桿壓力，可能放大市場跌勢：AI相關產業過去為全球股市主要
上漲動能，當市場風險偏好快速下降，基金同步減碼及風險控制策略調整部位，可能
使賣壓擴散至其他營運相對穩健的企業。11月 20日美國 10年期公債殖利率由前一
日約 5.04%降至 4.93%，VIX指數則升至約 37，顯示市場風險偏好明顯下降。半導體

類股同步重挫，反映資金正降低科技股集中曝險。當企業治理、政策及估值疑慮同時
存在時，短期股價可能出現超過基本面變化幅度的調整。
6. Fed升息預期維持高檔，科技股評價與融資成本承壓：Fed官員近期偏鷹談話，使市
場對 12月升息幅度及 2027年貨幣政策路徑的不確定性升高。高利率除壓低成長型企
業未來獲利的折現價值，亦增加資料中心營運商的利息支出。CoreWeave及 Nebius
等高度依賴外部融資的企業，對利率與信用市場變化尤其敏感。
三、 市場看法
1. 輝達調查事件提高評價不確定性，實質影響取決於調查範圍：本次新增風險在於市場
開始質疑企業財務資訊可信度，使投資人即使面對優於預期的財報，仍可能要求更高
的風險補償。若後續調查僅涉及財務揭露方式，對企業實質獲利的影響可能相對有限
；若涉及營收認列、財務數據修正或重大法律責任，則須重新評估輝達的獲利能力及
合理評價。短期應優先關注公司正式聲明、SEC調查進度及會計師相關意見。
2. AI需求尚未確認反轉，但短線修正風險仍高：輝達近期財報仍顯示先進運算設備需求

具一定支撐，尚不能單憑股價重挫認定 AI產業已進入衰退。後續除追蹤 Microsoft、
Amazon、Alphabet及 Meta等大型科技業者 2027年資本支出計畫，亦應檢視設備
交付、實際收款及終端運算需求。若資本支出維持成長且 AI服務收入持續增加，產業
中期基本面仍具支撐；反之，若客戶融資能力下降並導致訂單延後，則須留意修正由
評價面轉向基本面。
3. 半導體股同步重挫，不代表產業基本面將同步惡化：當市場避險需求急升時，投資人
可能優先降低整體半導體曝險，使基本面良好的企業亦受到波及。但中期而言，企業
獲利成長、技術競爭力、訂單能見度及自由現金流仍將決定股價表現。先進製程、先
進封裝與高階記憶體等產業仍具 AI需求支撐，惟須持續追蹤主要客戶資本支出與訂單
變化。
4. 美國對中 AI管制趨嚴，全球 AI投資需求可能出現區域性轉移：美國對中 AI出口管制
範圍擴大，短期可能增加第三國資料中心建置及半導體供應鏈出貨的不確定性，但不
必然代表全球 AI運算需求同步下降。若部分資料中心投資計畫因許可審查而延後，資

本支出可能逐步轉向美國及其他合規風險較低的市場，使不同地區及企業的受惠程度

出現差異。大型美系雲端業者若具備既有資料中心據點、充足資金及較完善的合規能
力，中期可能具有相對競爭優勢。後續應關注出口許可審查進度、大型雲端業者的區

5. AI信用風險重要性提高，尚不宜判定為全面性信用危機：大型雲端業者通常擁有成熟
業務及穩定營業現金流，新興資料中心營運商則較依賴外部融資。隨著市場開始關注
晶片供應商提供客戶信用支持的情況，未來除追蹤高槓桿企業，亦須檢視大型科技企
業的擔保責任及潛在財務曝險。我們認為，若 AI相關企業信用利差持續擴大，且風險

進一步擴散至整體投資級公司債市場，將是需要下修風險資產配置的重要訊號。
6. 避險需求支撐美債表現，短期尚須等待止穩訊號：隨著全球科技股賣壓加劇，投資人
避險需求明顯升高，帶動美國 10年期公債殖利率下跌約 10個基點，重新回落至 5%
以下。後續美國通膨、就業數據及 12月 FOMC會議仍將影響全球金融市場方向。若
Fed 緊縮幅度超出市場預期，科技股可能同時面臨折現率偏高及獲利成長放緩的壓力
。我們認為，市場真正止穩須觀察輝達財務疑慮是否釐清、半導體企業獲利預估是否
回穩、信用利差是否停止擴大，以及市場廣度與外資流向是否改善。在上述訊號尚未
明朗前，短期股市恐仍維持高波動。
四、 市場展望與投資建議：
1. 短線審慎，中期審慎偏正向，關注企業獲利與資金面變化：本波全球科技股修正已由
初期的評價調整，進一步擴大至企業治理、政策風險及資金面的全面性重新定價。尤
其費半指數單日重挫超過 10%，顯示市場風險偏好快速下降，短期賣壓尚未完全解除
。然而，目前尚無充分證據顯示 AI運算需求已出現全面性反轉，輝達最新財報及主要

CSP持續投入 AI基礎建設，仍可為產業中長期成長提供一定支撐。因此，我們對美國
科技股及具備 AI供應鏈優勢的台灣、日本市場，短期維持審慎，中期則採取審慎偏正
向的觀點。不過，科技股未來表現將更依賴實際獲利、訂單能見度及自由現金流，而
非單純的 AI題材。後續應持續關注輝達 SEC調查、CSP資本支出、Fed政策及信用
風險是否擴散；若相關疑慮逐步釐清、企業獲利預估保持穩定，股市可望逐漸回歸基
本面主導。策略上，對中長期仍看好科技產業的投資人，可透過以分批布局及分散類
股配置方式參與，在科技類股部分，應優先考量具備技術競爭力、穩定現金流與較佳
財務體質的企業，避免過度集中於高度依賴外部融資的 AI業者。
2. 美債避險功能重新受到重視，中天期風險報酬相對均衡：Fed升息預期仍使債券市場
面臨利率波動，但隨著全球股市風險偏好下降，美國公債的避險需求已明顯升高。11
月 20日美股重挫，10年期美債殖利率大幅下降，顯示部分資金轉向相對安全的固定
收益資產。儘管 Fed政策尚未明朗，且通膨仍可能造成殖利率反覆波動，但目前美國

公債殖利率仍具有一定吸引力與防禦價值。從資產類別角度來看，我們對中天期美債
維持相對正向看法，主要考量其殖利率水準及利率敏感度的風險報酬較為均衡；超長

市場焦點
美國
• 9月CPI年增3.7%、PPI年增6.3%，雙雙高於前期，能源價格的急升是通膨回升的主要推力。
雖然核心CPI年增率僅升至2.6%，但住房與服務價格仍維持黏著，使通膨降溫過程出現反覆；
同時PPI增幅明顯高於CPI，反映企業成本壓力偏高，未來不排除進一步向終端價格傳導。整
體而言，CPI與PPI同步回升反映通膨降溫進程仍存在不確定性，進而令市場重估通膨回落速
度與對Fed貨幣政策路徑的審慎預期。
AI
• AI雲端業者CoreWeave的5年期CDS利差一度突破1,140個基點，旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近
15%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延至股票市場。全球最大半導體設備商ASML表示，客戶已開始洽
談2029至2030年的訂單；晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期。與此同時，台灣出口增

速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季
相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。
中國
• 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及AI硬體需求，能源和科技領域之外，普遍性的通縮壓力
仍在，彭博表示以GDP平減指數衡量，經歷三年下滑後，整體經濟預計將在2026年走出通縮，
投資上短期可聚焦，受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。

美股獲利引擎轉向半導體/核心AI與能源
• 雖然標普500指數在2026年第三季展現出獲利韌性，但隨著油價上漲侵蝕利潤率、消費者受通膨壓抑，以及分析師下調未
來預測動能，市場未來的容錯空間正在縮小。
• AI與半導體仍為獲利領頭羊：科技與半導體持續為標普500指數獲利成長的核心動力，其中約20家企業組成的核心AI一籃子
股票(權重約34.6%)對指數獲利的貢獻度已超過科技七雄(Mag 7)，且此優勢預計將至少延續至2028年底。
• 油價推升能源產業，接棒成為強棒：受到近期國際油價上揚影響，能源產業獲利預測顯著上修，預計獲利成長勁道將延續至
2027年初。在接下來的三個季度中，能源板塊的收益成長表現預計將超越科技七雄。
• 消費與運輸業面臨成本壓頂：相較於科技與能源的強勁，必需消費品與交通運輸業正承受顯著壓力。例如，達美航空受高燃

料成本拖累，而Nike與百事可樂則反映出消費者因持續通膨而縮減支出的困境。

顧
利潤率高檔盤整、關稅退款帶來短補，但動能已見放緩
• 利潤率高檔盤整，淨利潤率提升受限：標普500成分股第二季營運利潤率(18.7%)與淨利潤率(15.6%)均創下新高。雖然第三
季營運利潤率預估可進一步提升至19.4%，但淨利潤率預計微幅上升至15.8%，顯示整體企業淨利潤的擴張動能正漸趨平緩。
• 700億美元關稅退款提供短線補藥：受惠於美國政府依據國際緊急經濟權力法(IEEPA)的追償退稅，估計仍有至少700億美元
的關稅款將退還給標普500成分股(主要集中於工業、非必需消費及醫療保健等)，這預計能為2026年標普500指數每股收益
(EPS)成長帶來約235個基點、利潤率提升約34個基點的短期提振。
• 上修動能開始轉弱：雖然整體財報指引動量仍維持正值，但過去六週內，分析師對未來12個月EPS與收入的修正動能出現頻

繁下修，這意味著市場先前的極度樂觀情緒正在消退，未來財報季度要優於預期的門檻正在提高。
• 小結與投資建議：標普500指數短期內靠著半導體/AI與能源雙引擎拉抬，加上關稅退款的一次性利多注入，下半年整體獲
利依然具備支撐。然而，高油價帶來的營運成本上升，以及終端消費力道的疲軟，正逐漸侵蝕非科技/非能源企業的利潤，
而大盤廣度不足(僅少數板塊支撐獲利)，代表未來投資風險正逐漸集中，但AI算力需求仍屬硬性需求，但波動加劇下，投資
建議以定期定額方式投資美股核心AI與半導體龍頭股票。

美國9月CPI、PPI 同步升溫，能源衝擊擴大通膨與利率風險
• 能源價格急升，消費通膨再升溫：美國9月CPI月增0.6%、
年增3.7%，高於前月3.4%，其中汽油月增9.1%、能源
價格月增5.2%，汽油單項即貢獻整體CPI當月漲幅逾半；
另一方面，核心CPI月增0.3%、年增2.6%、住房指數月
增0.4%，顯示能源雖是本次通膨回升主因，但住房及部
分服務價格仍具黏著性，令通膨降溫進程面臨反覆。
• 上游價格大幅攀升，成本傳導風險升高：9月PPI最終需
求月增1.3%、年增6.3%，創2022年3月以來最大增幅，
其中最終需求商品月增2.6%，能源價格大漲10.8%，汽
油價格更跳升20.3%；核心PPI月增0.5%、年增5.0%，
顯示價格壓力不僅來自能源，也涉及部分商品與服務環

節；後續是否進一步推升至CPI，仍須觀察企業成本轉
嫁程度及需求變化。
• 市場看法：通膨回升恐提高Fed再度升息機率，並延長
高利率維持時間，甚至不排除進一步緊縮的可能，短期
有利支撐美債殖利率及美元表現，不過股、債市後續走
勢仍將受到貨幣政策預期與風險情緒變化影響。資產配
置建議可關注具成本轉嫁能力、現金流穩健的能源類股；
債券則宜以短中天期、高信用評等標的為主，以降低利
率敏感度。黃金可作為分散地緣政治及通膨風險的輔助
配置，但不宜將任何單一資產視為必然受惠標的。
CPI 與核心 CPI 走勢變動
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics，Producer Price Indexes – Sep  2026，2026.10.16
季調後PPI最終需求價格指數月變動(%)
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.16

供給面推動中國9月CPI升0.9%
• 供給面推動中國9月通膨復甦：中國9月通膨數據顯示，CPI年增率由8月的0.8%升至0.9%，連續兩個月擴大，但低於市場預期
的1.0%，物價上漲主要受到能源及旅遊價格帶動，而食品價格跌幅進一步擴大，使整體通膨壓力仍相對溫和，扣除食品與能源
後，核心CPI維持在1.0%，反映內需需求尚未明顯升溫。另一方面，PPI年增率由3.8%大幅升至5.0%，優於市場預期，且月增
率達1.2%，創4月以來最大漲幅，顯示上游原物料及工業品價格回升，須關注未來是否逐步傳導至消費端價格。
• 展望後市：9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及AI硬體需求，能源和科技領域之外，普遍性的通縮壓力仍在，彭博表示以
GDP平減指數衡量，經歷三年下滑後，整體經濟預計將在2026年走出通縮，值得注意的是，這種供給面驅動的通膨復甦，將降

低官方推出需求刺激措施的緊迫感，而要反轉通縮循環正需要官方舉措，投資上短期可聚焦受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的
半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.14
通膨集中於能源和AI相關行業

過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數
的兩倍
• 過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數的兩倍：主因為獲利帶動成長，整體而言，新興市場企
業獲利表現強勁，而且仍持續加速成長，高盛預估2026年新興市場企業盈餘成長率將達 55%，超過標普500企業盈餘成長速
度的兩倍，且新興市場股票估值較美國股市存在超過40%的折價空間，評價相對具吸引力，今年以來流入新興市場股市的資金
已接近500億美元，創下過去20年來最強勁紀錄之一。更重要的是新興市場國家目前貢獻了全球近80%的AI硬體出口。隨著AI
產業持續發展，受惠範圍有望從少數龍頭企業擴大至更多國家及公司，特別是那些能夠協助解決供應鏈瓶頸的企業。
• 結論：後市來看在AI產業趨勢、相對低估的評價水準，以及強勁企業獲利成長三大因素支撐下，新興市場股票未來一年仍具備

進一步上漲的潛力，有機會延續目前的強勢表現。

AI投資支撐美國成長，融資需求升高使長端利率與信用風險並存
• AI基礎建設持續擴張，成為企業投資重要動能：大型雲端業者
持續投入資料中心、半導體、網路設備與電力基礎設施，帶動
相關產業資本支出與供應鏈需求。摩根大通資產管理指出，納
入資料中心開發商及半導體投資後，AI 基礎建設至 2030 年的
累計投資需求可能達約5兆美元，顯示AI投資循環仍具規模，
但實際經濟貢獻與企業獲利，仍取決於投資效率及商業化成果。
• 外部融資需求增加，債券供給與投資回報成關鍵：隨AI投資規
模擴大，大型科技企業逐步增加公司債與外部融資，在企業債
與公債供給同步增加下，長天期利率面臨上行壓力。若未來AI
獲利成長未能跟上資本支出擴張速度，可能壓縮自由現金流與
資本報酬率，進一步擴大信用利差，並提高高估值科技股的評

價修正風險
• 市場觀察：AI基礎建設仍是未來數年推動全球科技投資與企業
數位轉型的成長主軸，相關需求具備中長期發展空間，而2026
年市場已逐漸轉向檢視投資回報率、現金流創造能力及融資結
構是否具備可持續性，因此單純依賴資本支出擴張已不足以支
撐所有AI概念股評價。建議配置可兼顧具獲利與現金流支撐的
AI供應鏈，以及高信用評等、中短天期債券；對長天期債券則
須評估供給與利率風險，並審慎檢視高負債AI企業的償債能力
及投資回報。
資料來源：Goldman Sachs，永豐金證券， 2026.10.12

AI熱潮、高債務與網路威脅，IMF示警三大金融穩定風險升高
資料來源：Bloomberg Finance L.P， Dealogic，Zero day Clock and IMF staff calculations.， 2026.10.13
AI相關風險持續升溫 主權債券殖利率攀升 網路資安威脅升高
大型雲端科技企業債券發行規模
（占投資等級公司債市場比重) 10年期公債殖利率平均變動幅度 零時差漏洞占比（%）
• AI估值高度集中，市場重定價風險升溫：IMF於10月《全球金融穩定報告》指出，AI相關投資已成為全球成長的重要支撐，
但資金與漲勢高度集中於少數企業及市場，一旦獲利成長不如預期或估值遭重新檢視，可能引發科技股修正並透過風險偏好
下降擴散至整體金融市場。
• 通膨與財政疑慮恐推升債券市場波動：IMF認為，若能源價格推升通膨壓力或市場對財政永續性產生疑慮，主權債殖利率可

能進一步上升，進而提高全球融資成本與資產重估風險；在高利率與高債務環境下，金融條件快速緊縮的情況將明顯提高。
• 網路安全風險成為潛在威脅：隨金融體系數位化程度提高，IMF將大型網路攻擊列為重要風險來源之一。若攻擊事件波及支
付系統、金融基礎設施或雲端服務平台，不僅可能影響市場運作效率，也可能透過信心衝擊與流動性壓力擴大金融市場波動。
• 投資建議：市場成長動能仍受AI支撐，但在金融穩定風險上升情境下，建議採取「AI受惠股＋高品質債券＋防禦型資產」的
槓鈴策略；核心配置可聚焦全球AI基礎建設供應鏈及投資等級債，藉由企業長期獲利成長與債券避險功能，同時因應估值修
正及金融條件收緊風險。

AI隱憂浮現但動能仍在
• CoreWeave信用風險亮紅燈：市場憂心雲端業者在算力租賃業務產生足夠回報前，恐難以負擔龐大債務，導致信用風險升
溫。上週，AI雲端業者CoreWeave的5年期信用違約交換（CDS）利差一度突破1,140個基點，升至通常反映嚴重信用壓力
的水準；旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近15%，其中2032年到期的無擔保債券於上週三跌破面值的80%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延
至股票市場。
• AI半導體產業的基本面仍具韌性：全球最大半導體設備商艾司摩爾（ASML）表示，客戶已開始洽談2029至2030年的訂單；
晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期。與此同時，台灣出口增速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯
示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。

美銀美林調查指出，全球經理人對景氣與股市仍維持偏多立場
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10
• 經理人樂觀情緒維持高檔：美銀美林10月全球基金經理人調查顯示，市場風險偏好仍高，平均現金水位維持在3.9%的低檔，
科技股配置則升至2020年8月以來最高，反映經理人對AI產業及企業獲利前景仍具信心。然而，整體經濟成長預期已轉趨保
守，淨4%的受訪者預期未來12個月經濟成長放緩。極端風險方面，債券殖利率無序攀升仍居首位，占比31%；AI泡沫及通
膨再起分別為23%及20%。此外，39%的經理人認為，大型雲端業者資本支出是系統性信用事件的主要潛在來源，顯示市場
焦點已從AI需求成長延伸至龐大資本支出的獲利回收能力。
• 美國公債殖利率走高，債券的收益吸引力逐漸回升：調查顯示，37%的受訪者表示，當美國10年期公債殖利率升至6%時，

才會考慮由股票轉向債券；另有19%認為目前殖利率已具吸引力，顯示部分經理人已開始重新評估固定收益資產的配置價值。
不過，債券整體仍處淨減碼，且經理人對科技股的配置持續升高，反映市場尚未全面轉向防禦。後續仍須關注美債殖利率變
化、AI資本支出的投資回報，以及企業獲利能否支撐偏高的市場評價。
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10

台積電財報佳卻未激勵股價，短線面臨總經干擾
• 台積電Q3營收、獲利及EPS同步改寫單季新高：營收年增50.9%、季增17.6%，稅後純益7,630.6億元，年增68.7％、季增8
％；每股稅後純益(EPS)29.43元，整體優於市場預期，但毛利率66.4％較第二季的67.7％下降。
• 財測展望：第四季財測營收預估523~543億美元，季增上看15％、年增65％，毛利率預估65%~67%，反映高階晶片需求
依然強勁。全年展望方面，預期2026年美元營收年增約44%，較上季預估的「略高於40%」說法更加明確。此外，重申全
年資本支出600~640億美元不變。
• 市場反應：台積電法說會隔日股價小幅下跌，法說會未出現激勵行情，主要原因包含美國通膨升溫壓力、外資連續調節部位，

以及毛利率略為下降。短線籌碼面可能仍會調整，但台積電董事長魏哲家在法說會中強調，第二波AI需求正強勁興起，台積
電正透過多種途徑全力擴充產能，顯示高階晶片前景明確，中長期具投資價值，多家投資機構也紛紛上調目標價。
資料來源：台積電法說會，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.15。
法說會重點整理 說明
AI需求持續升溫
魏哲家坦言，目前仍難以判斷晶圓供需缺口將擴大或縮小，因為
客戶需求持續增加，台積電也正積極提高產能。儘管消費性電子
市場仍受到零組件漲價及總體經濟不確定性影響，但先進製程需
求依然強勁，公司預估2026年全年美元營收將年增約44％。
擴廠進度
(1)台南科學園區新建3奈米廠預計2027年下半年量產，並決定在
台南再增建一座3奈米廠，規畫2028年投入量產。
(2)美國亞利桑那州第二座晶圓廠已完成廠房興建，預計2027年
第三季量產，時程較原先規畫略為提前；日本第二座晶圓廠則將
導入3奈米製程，預計2028年量產。
台積電證實洽談Terafab
合作
魏哲家強調，目前相關合作仍在討論階段，尚未有結論。不過，

無論未來採取何種合作模式，晶圓廠都必須由台積電負責營運。
電力供應疑慮
美國電力供應情況是公司評估AI長期需求的重要因素之一，因此
台積電進一步確認當地電力供應狀況，目前相關研究及評估結果
均良好，尚未發現明顯問題。

• 全球經濟正處於「韌性仍在、風險升高」的階段：國際貨幣基金組織(IMF)指出，AI投資熱潮持續推動美國、台灣等國家成
長，但中東地緣政治衝突、高通膨風險、政府債務問題與AI泡沫化疑慮，也同時為市場埋下不確定因素。
• 市場永遠充滿雜音，定期定額紀律投資更具優勢：本許多人認為定期定額的核心在於「平均成本」，但更重要的是幫助投資
人克服人性。市場上漲時持續投入，可以參與企業獲利成長帶來的成果，市場下跌時持續扣款，則能以較低價格累積更多單
位數。換句話說，定期定額讓投資人不需要每天判斷：明天會不會跌？現在是不是太貴？要不要先停扣觀望？長期而言，紀

律投資往往比短期擇時的成效更佳。
• AI時代更適合定期定額：儘管IMF認為人工智慧發展為全球經濟成長帶來貢獻，但同時也警告，若市場對AI未來獲利能力過
度樂觀，不排除出現股市估值修正。根據歷史經驗，每一次市場重大修正往往也孕育下一輪成長契機，不論AI發展是否出現
泡沫，善用定期定額策略可以在機會與風險之間達到平衡。
韌性仍在、風險升溫，紀律投資更穩健

2. 本資料提及之經濟走勢預測不必然代表基金之績效，基金投資風險請詳閱基金公開說明書。
3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣(DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。

## Document 125: 261112_ml_wistron

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_ml_wistron.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Wistron

Reiterate Buy: 3Q26 beat, better margins; 
AI momentum intact; cut PO to NT$250

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 250.00 TWD | Price: 174.50 TWD

3Q26 beat: solid margins on favorable product/client mix 
Wistron’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 5.90%/4.24% came in above BofAe of 5.36%/3.66% and 
consensus of 5.55%/3.70%. We attribute the strong margins to greater sales scale 
(+28% QoQ, +102% YoY; 1%/8% above BofAe/consensus), more favorable product/client 
mix in AI business, and better results from Wiwynn, mainly supported by general/ASIC 
server momentum (see also Wiwynn 3Q26 result note). Thus, OP was 17%/24% above 
BofAe/consensus. With non-operating loss of NT$6.2bn (including net interest expense 
and FX loss), NP was 16%/27% above BofAe/consensus (Exhibit 3).  
AI order momentum intact; ongoing project ramp-ups 
We see no change in Wistron’s AI order momentum into 4Q26 and 2027-28. We expect 
4Q26/1Q27 sales to rise 11%/8% QoQ, driven by GPU/CPU rack project ramp-ups, with 
rising ASP and spec upgrade. We also see potential sales upside from more new 
client/project wins in 2H27-2028. Although the GM decline trend is inevitable for AI 
server ODMs, we expect Wistron to post solid core OPM (i.e. excluding Wiwynn) into 
2027-28, with rising operating efficiency and more diversified client/project base. On 10 
Nov, the company announced additional capex plans of subtotal around NT$12.8bn for 
adding facilities and equipment in Taiwan, mainly to support new AI-related businesses, 
and another US$17.8mn for expanding server manufacturing capacity in Mexico.  
Trim PO to NT$250 with a lower P/E set-up; reiterate Buy  
We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 7-21% (but 2027-28E EPS is still 8-9% below consensus) 
to reflect the 3Q26 results beat, solid AI momentum, and sustained core OPM. Yet, we 
lower our PO to NT$250 (12x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS) from NT$265 (15x 2H26-1H27E EPS), 
with the valuation base rollover and a lower P/E multiple. We now apply 12x (1SD above 
historical average since 2022; Exhibit 8), vs. prior 15x (1.5SD above) on valuation de-
rating across AI supply chain amid macro uncertainty. Nevertheless, we reiterate Buy on 
Wistron in view of its solid AI sales momentum with a broadening client/project base.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
Wistron is one of the top-5 ODMs globally. Server/storage 
and PC business accounted for 73% and 24% of sales in 
2025, respectively. Key server/storage customers include 
Dell, Lenovo, HPE, Cisco, Nvidia, AMD, Intel. Key NB 
customers include Dell, Lenovo, Acer. Wistron was spun off 
from Acer (2353 TT) in 2000 and was incorporated in May 
2001. It went publicly listed on the Taiwan stock exchange 
in Aug 2003. It is headquartered in Taipei, Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to Wistron in view of improving 
sales/profit mix, solid outlook from Wiwynn, high exposure 
to server business after exiting from low-margin iPhone 
assembly business, and improving core OPM. We believe 
Wistron will benefit from Dell's strong demand in Nvidia AI 
GPU/CPU rack as the key supplier.

### Page 7

Price objective basis & risk 
Wistron (WICOF) 
Our PO of NT$250 is based on 12x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS. We use 12x valuation multiple 
(around 1SD above historical P/E average since 2022) in view of high server mix, a key 
position in AI server supply chain with exposure both to component/rack business, and 
ongoing core OPM improvement.

Upside risks to our PO are (1) stronger server market demand, especially for AI, (2) 
stronger PC demand, (3) share/order win in AI server business, and (4) favorable FX 
trend.

Downside risks to our PO are (1) softer AI server demand due to AI GPU short supply or 
supply chain hiccup, (2) weaker PC market demand amid inflation/macro uncertainties, 
(3) GM pressure due to higher labor/logistics cost, (4) more intensified competition in 
NB and server business, and (5) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) 
to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating)

Buy ≥ 10% ≤ 70%

Underperform N/A ≥ 20%

INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend. Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a common industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

本刊載之報告為台新投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

雙鴻(3324)：3Q26 財報不如預期，營運逐季向上 
建議：維持買進

一、雙鴻為散熱解決方案供應商  雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，為國內前二大 NB 散熱模組廠，過去主要生產散熱
器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，現在已轉型為提供客戶完整散熱解
決方案供應商。主要應用於筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、智慧型手機及伺服器等領
域。3Q26 產品應用領域 Server 81%、PC 14%、VGA/Gaming 4%、及 Others(手
機及車用) 1%。另外 3Q26 水冷業務比重 62%(1H26 約為 55~60%)。

二、3Q26 毛利率 25.65%，低於
預期，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元 
 雙鴻 3Q26 EPS 11.59 元，低於台新預估的 14.13 元，獲利達成率 82%。本業
來看，3Q26 營收 97.87 億元，+12.5%QOQ，+64.3%YOY，低於市場預期之
+15~20%QOQ，七月營收有遞延認列，實際拉貨力道未如預期顯著，3Q26 毛
利率 25.65%，較 2Q26 再衰退 2.77 個百分點，亦低於台新預估的 30%，主要
是報廢品打消，預期 4Q26 毛利率回升，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元。雙鴻 10 月營收
33.72 億元，+17%MOM，+45%YOY，獲利達成率 31%，符合預期，預期 11 月
營收及 12 月營收將優於 10 月，預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，+12.8%QOQ，
+45.6%YOY，預估 4Q26 毛利率 28%，預估 4Q26 EPS 14.79 元。

三、營運逐季成長，氣冷及水冷
兩塊業務均樂觀 
 2025 年雙鴻水冷營收比重 35%，1Q26 提升至 55%，3Q26 提升至 62%，預期
2027 年將提升至 80%，帶動公司業績大幅增長。氣冷部分，2024 年月營收 2-
2.5 億元，2025 年 4～4.5 億元，2026 年平均 6～7 億元，氣冷水冷均樂觀，以
AWS TR3 來看，氣冷及水冷比重至少是 70~80%對比 20~30%，TR4 將為 100%
全水冷，水冷大趨勢不變，VR 9 月起量，VR 營收逐季增，TR3 9 月起量 Compute 
tray 及 Switch tray 及 Rack manifolds 都有相關產品出貨，預計 12 月單月 ASIC
營收比重有機會來到 15~25%，ASIC 水冷產值高於GPU 客戶，其中 Mata>AWS，
2027 年 ASIC 增長可期，公司預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU。2026 年可
視為 CDU 元年，2027 年營收跳增可期。台新預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

1 群益投顧 
精材(3374 TT)與 台 積 電 (2330 TT)簽 訂 先 進 封 裝 oS 產 能 外 包 協 議 ， 深 化 策 略 合 作 。 精
材 此 次 取 得 台 積 電 CoWoS 後段 oS 產 能 外 包 合 作 ， 對 長 期 業 務 結 構 升 級 具 正 面 意 義 ，
惟 股 價 於 公 告 前 已 連 續 6 個 交 易 日 上 漲 ， 累 計 漲 幅 約 40%，11/13/2026 收盤達 519 元
漲 停 ， 本 益 比 已 達 43 倍 ， 加 上 新 產 線 預 計 2029 年才開始第一期量產， 較 市 場 預 期
2027~2028 年量產晚， 短 期 獲 利 貢 獻 有 限 ， 且 仍 有 設 備 資 本 支 出 、 折 舊 及 CB 潛在稀
釋 風 險 ， 因 此 短 線 不 宜 追 價 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

台 積 電 擴 大 先 進 封 裝 外 包 布 局 ， 精 材 預 計 2029 年切入 CoWoS 後段 oS 製
程 ：  
精材(3374 TT) 於 11/13/2026 公 告 董 事 會 決 議 通 過 與 具 重 大 影 響 之 投 資 者 台
積電(2330 TT) 簽 訂 產 能 外 包 協 議 ， 契 約 期 間 自 2026/11/13 至 2033/12/31，
雙 方 將 針 對 先 進 封 裝 後 段 製 程 展 開 長 期 策 略 合 作。根 據 公 告 內 容，精 材 計 畫
進駐台積電中部科學園區廠房建置產線並負責生產營運，由台積電提供廠
房空間及先進封裝技術與製造能力支援，精材則自行投入生產設備及自動
物 料 搬 運 系 統 (AMHS)資 本 支 出，切 入 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈 中 的 oS(on-Substrate)
模 組 。 該 產 線 規 劃 於 2029 年 實 現 第 一 期 量 產 ， 雙 方 並 將 共 同 確 保 新 產 線 的
技 術 規 範、品 質 及 良 率 符 合 台 積 電 與 客 戶 要 求。此 外，精 材 董 事 會 同 步 通 過
發 行 新 台 幣 150 億 元 可 轉 換 公 司 債 (CB)， 以 支 應 初 期 產 能 建 置 需 求 。  
既 有 台 積 電 合 作 關 係 延 伸 至 先 進 封 裝 ， 有 助 精 材 拓 展 AI 及 HPC 業務：  
就 精 材 本 身 而 言，此 次 合 作 是 既 有 台 積 電 供 應 鏈 關 係 的 深 化。精 材 原 以 CIS
封 裝 及 晶 圓 級 封 裝 (WLCSP) 為 主 要 業 務，並 承 接 台 積 電 晶 圓 測 試 (CP)外 包 訂
單 ， 惟 既 有 測 試 機 台 於 08/2026 已 達 滿 載 ， 且 受 限 於 無 新 增 無 塵 室 空 間 ， 部
分 台 積 電 測 試 訂 單 轉 由 欣 銓 (3264 TT)承 接。此 次 透 過 進 駐 台 積 電 中 科 廠 區，
精材得以突破自身廠房空間限制，免除購地建廠所需的前置時間與土地成
本，並 利 用 台 積 電 既 有 廠 區 及 技 術 支 援，降 低 新 產 線 建 置 與 製 程 導 入 的 部 分
門 檻。由 於 台 積 電 目 前 持 有 精 材 約 41%股 權，雙 方 已 有 長 期 合 作 基 礎，此 次
進 一 步 由 晶 圓 測 試 及 既 有 封 裝 業 務 延 伸 至 CoWoS 後段 oS 製 程 ， 有 助 精 材
擴 大 與 台 積 電 的 合 作 範 圍，並 提 高 未 來 AI 及 HPC 相 關 業 務 的 營 收 占 比。不
過，CP 測 試 與 oS 屬 不 同 製 程，仍 須 觀 察 新 產 線 設 備 裝 機、製 程 驗 證 及 良 率
爬 升 進 度 。  
台 積 電 擴 大 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈 分 工 ， Interposer 與 oS 同 步 建 立 外 部 產 能 ：  
此 次 精 材 切 入 CoWoS 後段 oS 製 程 ， 應 放 在 台 積 電 持 續 擴 大 先 進 封 裝 供 應
鏈 分 工 的 整 體 策 略 下 觀 察 。 隨 AI 與 HPC 晶片朝 Chiplet、 HBM 及 異 質 整 合
發展，CoWoS 不 同 製 程 環 節 均 面 臨 產 能 擴 充 需 求 。 台 積 電 除 持 續 擴 充 自 身
先 進 封 裝 產 能 外，亦 逐 步 透 過 外 部 合 作 夥 伴 增 加 不 同 環 節 的 供 應 能 力，例 如
Interposer 方 面 與 GlobalFoundries 合 作 建 立 外 部 供 應 來 源，並 透 過 世 界 先 進
等 業 者 擴 充 相 關 產 能；後 段 先 進 封 裝 則 持 續 擴 大 與 OSAT 的 合 作，此 次 再 由
持股約 41%的 精 材 規 劃 於 台 積 電 中 科 廠 區 建 置 oS 產 線 ， 顯 示 台 積 電 正 逐 步
建 立 結 合 關 係 企 業 與 外 部 供 應 商 的 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈，以 提 高 產 能 配 置 彈 性。 
不 同 製 程 環 節 的 外 包 條 件 與 策 略 並 不 相 同 。 Interposer 外 包 主 要 在 於 增 加 供
應 來 源 及 產 能 彈 性；CoWoS 則 可 區 分 為 CoW 與 oS 兩 個 階 段，其 中 CoW 涉
及 晶 圓、矽 中 介 層 及 晶 片 間 的 高 精 度 整 合，製 程 複 雜 度 與 良 率 要 求 較 高，外

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

2 群益投顧 
包 仍 受 技 術 及 良 率 條 件 限 制；相 較 之 下，oS 主 要 負 責 將 完 成 的 CoW 模 組 接
合 至 基 板，隨 OSAT 業 者 製 程 經 驗 累 積 及 良 率 逐 步 成 熟，已 成 為 較 適 合 擴 大
委 外 的 環 節 ， 目 前 台 積 電 CoWoS 後段 oS 製 程 已 大 量 採 用 外 包 模 式 。 由 於
OSAT 的 先 進 封 裝 毛 利 率 通 常 低 於 台 積 電 整 體 製 造 業 務 ， 將 部 分 oS 產 能 交
由 外 部 業 者 承 接，亦 有 助 台 積 電 將 有 限 的 無 塵 室 空 間、設 備 及 工 程 資 源 優 先
配 置 於 技 術 門 檻 較 高 的 CoW 及 次 世 代 先 進 封 裝 製 程 。  
精材 2029 年 才 量 產 ， 2026～ 2028 年 既 有 OSAT 仍 持 續 受 惠 AI 封 裝 需 求 ：  
精 材 新 產 線 預 計 2029 年 才 開 始 第 一 期 量 產，因 此 在 2026～ 2028 年 間，並 不
會立即改變既有封測業者的供需格局。台積電雖可依製程整合需求向客戶
建 議 合 作 封 測 廠，但 客 戶 仍 可 依 自 身 需 求 直 接 與 封 測 業 者 洽 談 產 能 及 合 作，
因 此 先 進 封 裝 訂 單 並 非 完 全 由 台 積 電 單 方 面 分 配。在 AI/HPC 晶 片 持 續 推 動
先 進 封 裝 規 格 升 級 下，日 月 光 投 控、力 成 及 其 他 既 有 封 測 業 者 仍 可 持 續 受 惠
於新增外包需求，並透過製程改善、良率提升及設備效率優化增加有效產
出。 
至 2029 年 後 ， 精 材 oS 新 產 線 及 其 他 業 者 新 增 產 能 將 陸 續 開 出 ， 加 上 既 有
OSAT 持 續 透 過 良 率 改 善 增 加 有 效 產 能 ， 供 應 鏈 競 爭 可 能 逐 步 加 劇 。 因 此 ，
中 長 期 仍 須 觀 察 AI/HPC 需 求 成 長 能 否 持 續 吸 收 新 增 設 備 及 良 率 提 升 所 帶
來的有效供給，這將是影響先進封裝產業供需及各業者獲利表現的重要變
數。 
2029 年 後 新 增 有 效 產 能 可 能 增 加 ， 精 材 仍 面 臨 資 本 支 出 與 股 本 稀 釋 壓 力 ：  
雖然 AI/HPC 晶 片 持 續 朝 先 進 製 程、Chiplet 及 異 質 整 合 發 展，將 支 撐 先 進 封
裝 長 期 需 求 ， 但 目 前 CoWoS 供 不 應 求 ， 除 AI/HPC 需 求 快 速 成 長 、 晶 片 封
裝 面 積 擴 大 及 HBM 整 合 增 加 外 ， 製 程 複 雜 度 高 、 良 率 尚 待 提 升 ， 亦 可 能 限
制 有 效 產 能 釋 出。值 得 留 意 的 是，隨 製 程 技 術 持 續 改 進、設 備 調 校 及 量 產 經
驗 累 積，既 有 產 線 良 率 若 逐 步 提 升，即 使 未 新 增 設 備，也 可 能 增 加 有 效 產 出；
若 再 加 上 台 積 電 及 全 球 OSAT 業 者 持 續 擴 充 產 能 ， 未 來 供 給 增 幅 可 能 高 於
市 場 目 前 預 期 。 因 此 ， 現 階 段 CoWoS 供 不 應 求 未 必 能 長 期 延 續 ， 若 良 率 瓶
頸 逐 步 突 破，供 需 可 能 由 緊 俏 轉 向 平 衡，甚 至 出 現 供 給 較 為 充 裕 的 情 況，後
續 仍 須 觀 察 AI/HPC 需 求 成 長 能 否 吸 收 新 增 設 備 及 良 率 改 善 所 帶 來 的 有 效
供給。  
此 外 ， 精 材 須 自 行 負 擔 設 備 及 AMHS 資 本 支 出 ， 設 備 投 資 至 新 產 線 營 收 貢
獻 仍 有 時 間 落 差，設 備 陸 續 投 入 使 用 後 亦 將 增 加 折 舊 費 用，若 初 期 產 能 利 用
率 及 良 率 爬 升 不 如 預 期，可 能 對 毛 利 率 及 營 業 利 益 形 成 壓 力。資 金 方 面，精
材截至 11/2026 股 本 為 27.14 億 元 ， 約 當 2.714 億 股 ， 預 計 發 行 150 億元
CB， 目 前 尚 未 確 定 轉 換 價 格 ； 若 以 11/13/2026 收 盤 價 519 元 作 為 假 設 轉 換
價格，並以全數轉換估算，新增股數約 2,890 萬 股 ， 相 較 目 前 股 數 增 加 約
10.65%， 轉 換 後 原 股 東 持 股 比 例 將 稀 釋 約 9.63%。 惟 此 為 假 設 情 境 ， 實 際 稀
釋 程 度 仍 須 視 最 終 發 行 金 額、轉 換 價 格 及 轉 換 條 件 而 定。因 此，此 次 合 作 雖
有助精材長期業務結構升級，但仍須考量資本支出回收效益、折舊吸收能
力、CB 潛 在 稀 釋 及 未 來 產 業 供 需 變 化 ， 不 宜 直 接 以 目 前 CoWoS 供 不 應 求
的 狀 況 外 推 未 來 獲 利 。

3 群益投顧 
精 材 短 線 評 價 已 反 映 部 分 成 長 預 期 ， 封 測 股 則 可 留 意 修 正 後 的 布 局 機 會 ：  
精 材 此 次 取 得 台 積 電 CoWoS 後段 oS 產 能 外 包 合 作 ， 對 長 期 業 務 結 構 升 級
具 正 面 意 義 ， 惟 股 價 於 公 告 前 已 連 續 6 個 交 易 日 上 漲 ， 累 計 漲 幅 約 40%，
11/13/2026 收 盤 達 519 元 漲 停，本 益 比 已 達 43 倍，加 上 新 產 線 預 計 2029 年
才 開 始 第 一 期 量 產 ， 短 期 獲 利 貢 獻 有 限 ， 且 仍 有 設 備 資 本 支 出 、 折 舊 及 CB
潛 在 稀 釋 風 險 ， 因 此 短 線 不 宜 追 價 。  
另一方面，不宜因精材取得台積電合作，就立即下修日月光投控(3711 TT)
及力成(6239 TT) 的 中 短 期 投 資 看 法 。 2026 年至 2028 年 精 材 新 產 線 尚 未 量
產，既 有 封 測 廠 仍 可 持 續 承 接 AI 及 HPC 先 進 封 裝 與 測 試 外 溢 訂 單，並 透 過
產 線 效 率 及 良 率 改 善 提 升 有 效 產 出，中 短 期 應 以 訂 單 能 見 度、產 能 利 用 率 及
獲 利 表 現 作 為 主 要 觀 察 重 點。4Q26 以 來，部 分 封 測 股 已 因 市 場 對 AI 資 本 支
出 及 投 資 效 益 的 疑 慮 而 大 幅 修 正，若 後 續 訂 單 及 營 運 展 望 未 明 顯 轉 弱，評 價
修正反而可能提供布局機會，可持續留意受惠先進封裝需求的日月光投控
(3711 TT) 、力成(6239 TT)， 以 及 承 接 晶 圓 測 試 外 溢 需 求 的 欣 銓 (3264 TT)。
至 2029 年 後 ， 隨 精 材 及 其 他 業 者 新 增 產 能 陸 續 開 出 ， 加 上 既 有 產 線 良 率 提
升 帶 動 有 效 供 給 增 加 ， 則 須 留 意 供 需 變 化 及 競 爭 加 劇 風 險 。

4 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】  
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 ≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <15%

中立(Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等  
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整  
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】  
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為 四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等 係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。   
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」 及 「 區 間 操 作 」 均有 upside 目 標 價 。 差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。  
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ：  
1. 達 目 標 價 。  
2. 雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。  
3. 推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】 
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 3Q26 OP 80.1조원 실적 서프라이즈 예상  
3Q26 영업이익: 상반기와 다르다. 억제 요인들의 추세 반전 전환 
SK하이닉스의 3Q26 영업이익은 전분기 대비 32% 증가한 80.1조원으로 시장기대
치를 능가할 전망이다. 최근 원화강세 흐름 및 상반기 동사의 상대적 실적개선 속도 
열위에 3Q26 영업이익 추정치는 70조원까지 내려갔다 (컨센서스 74조원). 하지만 
이제 동사 출하량 및 판가의 탄력적 복원세가 경쟁사 보다 빠른 실적 개선을 이끌 전
망이다. 3Q26 DRAM과 NAND 출하량/판가 증감률은 각각 +13%/+22%, 
+4%/+15% 수준으로, 각 영업이익은 63조원/17조원으로 예상된다 (OPM 각각 
84%/69% 추정). 그간의 상대적 실적 열위는 이제 반전된다. 이는 1) 판가 전략에 
있어 중장기적 관점에서 고객 중심 유연성 극대화를 꾀했던 부분의 보상적 되돌림이 
발생하고, 2) 업계에서 제일 높은 HBM 판매 비중으로 인해 판가 상승률에 발목 잡혔
지만, 오히려 HBM4 본격 공급이 시작되며 이익기여가 본격화된다. 아울러 3) 모바
일 핵심고객과의 기성 계약이 산업 전반적 LTA 논의로 재검토되며 공급사에 크게 유
리한 조건으로 변화되어 가기 시작한다. 당사는 1Q27부터 모바일 메모리 판가 급등
을 전망하며 (그림1), 2026E/2027E 영업이익의 경우 264조원/454조원으로 상향조정한
다. 4Q26 내 시장 실적 추정치 상향조정 및 투자자 수급의 구조적 개선을 전망한다.

‘지능기술’ 시대, 수요의 가격 역탄력성 속 중장기 기회창출에 주목 
과거 B2C 사이클과 다르게, 금번 B2B 사이클은 수요의 가격 역탄력성을 보이고 있
다. AGI 도달을 위한 선착순 투자가 핵심이기 때문이다. 빅테크 중 낙오자가 발생하
기 전까지 경쟁적 대규모 투자는 지속된다. 최근 동사의 솔리다임 상장 추진보도에 
일부 우려가 제기되고 있지만, 투자자들은 그 이면의 중장기적 기회에 주목해야 한다. 
동사는 LTA 외에도, 다양한 파트너십을 추진하며 중장기 시장 예측력을 증가시키고 
있다. 솔리다임 상장 과정에서 유입되는 현금은 모회사 ‘SK AI co’의 지분교환 및 JV 
구축 등에 활용되리라 예상된다. 이는 중장기 수요를 ‘창출’시키는 개념이다.

순수한 주주가치 제고 목적 환원 재도출 예상. 적정주가 상향조정 
빠르게 증가한 현금흐름 중 ‘잉여’분은 주주가치 ‘회복’에 우선 배분돼야 함이 마땅하
며, 동사 역시 최근 공시를 통해 주주가치 제고와 주가의 저평가 인식을 드러냈다. 현
재 진행중인 40조원 자사주 매입 프로그램 종료 후 즉각적인 신규 환원 정책 도출이 
예상된다. 2027년 메모리 공급부족은 명확하다. 역사적으로 효율적 시장 내, 주가 조
정은 탄력적 복원으로 귀결되어왔다. 동사에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하며, 적정주
가의 경우 320만원으로 상향한다 (26E/27E 평균 P/B 4.0배 적용). 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 12

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준)

## Document 240: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것삼성전자(005930) 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

기업분석 2026.10.30 www.daishin.com  
  Results Comment 
삼성전자 
(005930) 
 결국 제자리를 찾아갈 것 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 500,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
231,000 
반도체업종

◼ 3분기 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 
◼ 주주환원은 시점의 문제일 뿐. 강화 방향성은 여전히 유효. 
◼ 메모리반도체 이익의 폭증이 가격의 매력을 유발. 비중확대 전략 유효.

매력적인 가격, 변하지 않은 주주환원 정책의 방향성 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 메모리반도체 주도 폭발적 이익 상승이 가격 (주
가)의 매력을 지속 유발하고 있고, 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향성에도 변한 것
은 없다는 판단. 절대적으로 싼 주가를 비중 확대의 기회로 삼을 필요. 
1. 주주환원, 시점의 문제 
금번 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 대외 불확실성이 큰 만큼, 
시장은 자사주 매입/소각과 같은 즉각적 주가 부양 정책의 발표를 기대.  
당사는 삼성전자의 주주환원이 “시점의 문제”라 생각. 총 주주환원의 규모도, 
차기 (2027-2029년) 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향에도 변한 것은 없다는 판
단. 시장이 기대하는 자사주 매입/소각은 우선주 중심으로 시행될 가능성이 
높으며, 시행 시, 그 규모는 25조원 내외일 것 (DPS 12,567원)으로 전망. 
2. 메모리반도체, 더 올라갈 것 
보수적 환율 전망 (2027년 평균 환율 1,300원 적용)에도, 2027년 메모리반도
체 영업이익은 608조원에 달할 것. 이익 전망의 상향 여력 또한 유효. 
1) 최대 생산, 최대 판매 
삼성전자는 금일 4Q26 DRAM 판매량 가이던스로 전분기 대비 10% 중후반 
성장 (q-q +18%로 추정)을 제시. 일부 투자자들은 높아진 기저 부담으로 
2027년 판매 성장이 제한될 것을 우려. 당사는 높아진 기저에도 2027년 
DRAM 판매가 전년대비 20% 상승할 것으로 전망. 
- 극단적 수급 불균형 속, 삼성전자는 “최대 생산, 최대 판매” 정책을 시행 
중. 삼성전자만의 강력한 Execution이 20% 대 성장을 유지시킬 원동력. 
- DRAM 1c 수율의 조기 개선 속, 범용 DRAM 양산에 DRAM 1c Capa가 일
부 투입될 것으로 예상. Bit 성장을 높일 수 있는 변화가 될 것. 
2) 열려 있는 가격의 상방 
1Q27 모바일 DRAM에서 Surprise가 발생할 것이라는 의견을 유지 (전분기 
대비 30% 이상 상승). 장기계약이 체결된 CSP향으로도 가격의 추가 상승 여
력이 존재한다는 점을 상기할 필요. CSP의 경우, 당장의 비용 부담보다 AI 시
장 선점을 우선시. 장기계약으로 형성된 가격 Band의 천장 (Ceiling) 또한 시
장 우려 대비 낮지 않음을 감안할 필요. 2027년 범용 DRAM ASP는 전년대비 
40% 중후반까지 상승할 가능성 (현 전망: 전년대비 34% 상승).  
3) HBM, 제품 경쟁력과 점유율의 동반 성장  
최근 삼성전자향으로 HBM4/HBM4e 추가 공급 요청이 쇄도하고 있는 것으
로 추정. 일부 고객은 연내 추가 공급 시 2027년 계약 가격을 선제 지급할 
의사를 표출. 제품 경쟁력이 회복되었음을 보여주는 증거라 생각. 
제품 경쟁력의 회복과 최대 Capa의 이점 속, 2027년은 HBM 점유율 1위를 
확보하는 기념비적 해가 될 것으로 전망 (매출액 기준 점유율 38% 예상).

## Document 241: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
아직 수요의 끝은 보이지 않는다 
AI 투자에 대한 우려의 목소리도 있지만 정작 부품을 공급하고 투자하는 쪽의 목소
리는 시간이 갈수록 더 커지고 있다. 공급 부족의 시점 매 분기 뒤로 밀려나고 있기 
때문이다. 28년까지 공급부족이라고 했던 메모리업체들은 이구동성으로 30년까지 공
급부족이 지속된다고 언급하고 있다. 이러한 변화는 실적에 고스란히 반영되고 있
다. 26년 3분기 메모리 영업이익만 108.5조원으로 추정한다. 4분기에는 140조원에 
이를 것으로 전망한다. 
HBM4와 QLC는 차별화 포인트 
삼성전자는 HBM에서 입지가 좋았던 적이 없었다. 점유율, 수익성, 가동률, 제품 성
능, 고객 등 모든 항목에서 경쟁사 대비 열위에 있었다. HBM4는 모든 항목에서 입
지를 개선시킬 변수가 될 것으로 예상한다. 26년 3분기 물량은 경쟁사 수준일 것으
로 보이고 연간 물량도 유사한 수준일 전망이다. 27년에는 가격 인상과 가동률 상승
으로 수익성도 크게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. QLC는 TLC와 함께 AI 모델 진화의 
수혜를 받을 제품으로 빠르게 부상하고 있다. 메타의 Muse 영향이다. 접속자당 가
상 메모리를 NAND에 100GB를 할당하고 있기 때문이다.  
부족한 주주환원으로 투자 우선 순위에서 밀릴 듯 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 
변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, HBM 
시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 
판단하기 때문이다. 목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황
을 고려하면 적정 주가는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치
에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 
제한적일 것으로 판단하기 때문이다.

### Page 2

삼성전자 (005930)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기 매출액은 195.3조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.9% 증가한 195.3조원이다. VD/
가전을 제외한 주요 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 증가했다. DS, MX 모두 물량보다는 
가격 효과가 더 크게 작용한 것으로 분석된다. 사업부별로는  
1) DS 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 15.4% 증가한 147.1조원이다. 메모리
는 26년 2분기 대비 16.4% 증가했다. 26년 3분기 DRAM B/G(Bit Growth)
는 +2.0%, ASP는 +20.0%이다. NAND B/G는 +5.0%, ASP는 +18.0%이다. 
HBM 물량 증가는 HBM4가 주도했고, 그 이하 제품 비중은 하락한 것으로 
추정한다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 7.1% 증가한 8조원이다. 소형
은 해외 고객 및 국내 고객의 신제품 출시 영향으로 ASP 상승, 물량 증가 
효과가 동반되었고, 대형은 게이밍 모니터 비중을 확대했다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.4% 증가한 37.7조원이
다. MX는 15.5% 증가했다. 물량은 소폭 감소했고, ASP 상승 영향을 받았다. 
네트워크 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 60% 감소했다.  
4) VD/가전 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 4.1% 감소한 13.9조원이다. VD 
매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 6.5% 감소했다. 가전은 26년 2분기 대비 1.3% 
감소했다.

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │3 
26년 3분기 영업이익은 107.4조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 20% 증가한 107.4조원이다. DS,  
디스플레이와 MX는 2분기 대비 증가했고, VD/가전은 2분기 대비 감소했다. 사업부별
로는 
1) DS 사업부는 26년 2분기 대비 19.4% 증가한 106.5조원이다. 2분기 대비 영
업이익률은 소폭 상승했다. 메모리 영업이익은 2분기 대비 19.0% 증가했고, 
비메모리는 영업적자를 유지했다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 54.5% 증가한 1.1조원이다. 
중소형 신제품 효과로 수익성이 개선된 영향이다. 대형도 믹스 개선으로 수
익성이 개선되었다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자폭이 감소했다. 물량
은 줄었지만 High End 비중 상승으로 ASP는 개선되었고, 원가 비중이 낮아
진 영향이다. 
4) VD/가전 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자 규모가 커졌다.

### Page 6

삼성전자 (005930)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기 매출액은 226.5조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 16.0% 증가한 226.5조원으로 예상
한다. DS가 증가하고 나머지는 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS는 메모리가 성장을 주도할 것으로 기대한다. 가격과 물량 모두 3분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 전망한다. Context Server에 대한 수요가 증가하고, Personal Agent의 가상메
모리 설정으로 인해 NAND 수요가 증가하면서 QLC에 대한 수요 증가 속도가 빨라질 
전망이다. 이로 인해 NAND Bit Growth는 경쟁사와 차별화된 수준일 것으로 예상한
다.  
HBM 물량도 꾸준히 증가할 것으로 기대한다. 3분기에도 HBM4를 중심으로 물량이 
크게 증가했다. HBM4는 SK하이닉스와 비슷한 물량일 것으로 예상한다. 아직 전체 
물량은 SK하이닉스가 더 많은 상황이고, HBM 내 HBM4 비중은 업계 최고 수준인 것
으로 판단한다. 26년까지는 HBM이 수익성에는 마이너스 요인이지만, 27년에는 
DRAM 수준의 영업이익이 가능할 것으로 기대한다.  
MX는 비수기 저가 라인 물량 조정으로 전체 물량은 감소하지만, 제품믹스는 개선되
어 판가는 3분기 대비 상승할 것으로 예상한다.

26년 4분기 영업이익은 138.2조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 28.7% 증가한 138.2조원으로 예
상한다. DS 사업부를 제외하고 모두 3분기 대비 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS 사업부 영업이익은 3분기 대비 30% 증가한 138.5조원으로 전망한다. DRAM, 
NAND 가격이 10% 초중반 수준으로 상승하고, 물량은 10% 중반 이상 증가하면서 3
분기 대비 영업이익률 개선을 기대하기 때문이다. HBM 물량 증가가 이익 개선에 미
치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상한다.  
디스플레이, MX는 물량 부진으로 3분기 대비 영업이익은 부진할 것으로 전망한다.

DS가 성장 주도 
DS만 보자

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
Check Point 
1. 모자란 듯한 주주환원 
26년 3분기 실적 발표에서 밝힌 주주환원 정책은 지난 8월의 내용과 다르지 않다. 3
분기 배당 30조원(주당 4,601원)을 확정한 정도이다. 잔여 배당 재원이 55~75조원으
로 구체화했고, 현금 배당과 자사주 매입을 병행하겠다고 밝혔다.  
자사주 매입 시 우선주 매입도 고려 대상이라고 언급한 것은 고무적이지만 27년부터 
새롭게 시작되는 주주환원 정책을 결정하기 위한 이사회 개최는 27년 1월에 열릴 것
으로 예고하였기에 연내 추가적인 주주환원 발표는 없을 것으로 판단한다.  
2. HBM4로 HBM 시장에서 입지 강화 
HBM4를 통해서 자신감을 회복하고 있는 것으로 판단한다. 양산 시점은 경쟁사 대비 
빨랐고, 3분기 물량은 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준인 것으로 추정한다. HBM3e가 부진했기 
때문에 전체 생산량에서 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 가장 높은 수준일 것으로 추정한다.  
26년 전체 물량도 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준이 될 것으로 전망한다. 삼성전자는 HBM 시
장 점유율을 DRAM 수준으로 맞추겠다는 목표를 3분기에 달성한 것으로 밝혔다.  
물량이 증가하면서 가동률도 빠르게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. 아직은 DRAM과 영업
이익률에서 큰 차이가 있지만 27년에는 인상된 가격, 높아진 수율, 가동률 개선으로 
DRAM과 영업이익률 차이를 좁힐 수 있을 것으로 기대한다.  
3. QLC NAND 시장 확대 
AI 컴퓨팅 확산은 NAND에게도 성장 기회이다. AI 모델들의 Context Window가 길어
져 KV Cache 메모리에 대한 수요가 커지면서 High Endurance TLC 수요가 급증하고 
있다. DRAM을 대체할 수준의 NAND가 필요하게 되었기 때문이다.  
이 뿐만 아니라 고용량 NAND에 대한 수요도 Personal Agent의 출현으로 급속히 증
가할 것으로 기대한다. 여기에는 QLC NAND가 주로 사용된다. 메타의 Muse는 접속
자 1인에 대해서 DRAM 8GB, NAND 100GB의 가상 메모리를 설정하고 운영한다.  
Agent AI의 확산은 스토리지에 대한 수요도 급격히 확대할 것으로 기대하고, 삼성전
자에게도 기회가 될 것으로 기대한다.  
26년 4분기 NAND Bit Growth는 삼성전자가 가장 높을 것으로 예상한다.

삼성전자 (005930)

8 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 350,000원으로 하향 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 
1) 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 
2) 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, 
3) HBM 시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 
4) 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 판단하기 때문이다. 
목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황을 고려하면 적정 주가
는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사
와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 제한적일 것으로 판단하기 
때문이다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

## Document 257: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
It was already our view that the RBA was likely done hiking
the cash rate after the November increase. Since then, our
conviction on that call has strengthened. Later today we
will publish the November Market Outlook with revised
forecasts. GDP growth, the labour market and housing

market have all been revised down in 2027.
Underlying inflation has been revised down slightly in the
second half of 2027, bringing the year-ended figure for
calendar 2027 more decisively inside the RBA’s 2–3% target
range than our previous forecast round. The unemployment
rate forecast has been revised up to peak above 5%. Based on
this revised forecast profile, an increase in the cash rate from
here would be unlikely and – we would argue – undesirable.

A range of considerations underlie this forecast revision. The
housing market has undershot our forecasts over the past
couple of months. Turnover and financing activity have been
coming in under our earlier expectations. Mortgage loan
inquiries have taken another leg down in October after the
September rate hike, after finding something of a level in prior
months, with the additional impact of the November rate hike
still to come.
We are also seeing early signs of a turn in the consumer.

Consumer income and spending have so far been quite
resilient to pressures from fuel prices and rising interest
rates. But our Westpac Card Tracker is starting to show that
discretionary services spending is turning down again in Q4,
joining the discretionary goods category that was already quite
weak. We would not over-emphasise this point, but it does
imply that the risks of further surprising resilience are fading.
Subsequent to the November Monetary Policy Board (MPB)

meeting, business survey data has shown weaker conditions
and a sharp decline in forward orders. They also show
further evidence that current inflation is mostly a non-
labour cost shock that is not being fully passed through to
consumer prices.
As we noted following the November MPB meeting, oil
and fuel prices are already materially lower than the track
assumed in the RBA’s forecasts. Dated Brent is nearly one-
fourth lower than on 28 October, the date the RBA’s forecasts

were finalised, and spot Brent is around 15% lower. This
significantly lowers the risk of a new wave of pass-through of
fuel costs to other prices, as well as easing consumer inflation
perceptions and expectations.
An RBA peak of greater conviction
Forecast changes
• A full update to our forecasts has been published in our
November Market Outlook.
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
In the media conference following the November meeting,
Governor Bullock flagged that it was a good time to “take

stock” of the policy tightening to date. Both we and the
market read this as a signal that the MPB would pause at the
December meeting. The 6:3 split vote at the meeting also
suggests that some external members are more attentive to
downside risks than the internal members.
A rate hike in February 2027 cannot be ruled out but it is
looking even less likely given signs in the data and surveys.
The MPB will at least discuss a hike in February. The staff and

senior officials remain sensitive to inflation risks and have a
downbeat view of supply capacity growth.
Given what we are seeing in the economy now, though,
a rate hike in 2027 would require either a material new
inflation shock that has not yet occurred, or would
constitute a policy error on the part of the RBA (something
that we will never forecast as a base case, knowing the talents
and capabilities of the staff and the Board). We concur with

the dissenting voters in the MPB that the downside risks need
more attention.
Policy is clearly restrictive, and the risk of overdoing the
inflation correction is growing. And by the time we get to
February, we think there will be more evidence that the
domestic economy is slowing materially, and the inflation
outlook with it.
“A deeper housing downturn and early signs of
cracks in the consumer sector point to an even
softer 2027."

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: cost of living front and centre
the labour market while showing significant concern over
the strength and persistence of inflation, focusing on the
passthrough of energy and technology input inflation. His
conclusion for policy was clear: "if, as I expect, the monthly

rate of inflation improves in October, I am leaning toward
supporting an increase in our policy rate of 25 basis points
at the FOMC's December meeting"; but "if the October CPI
inflation data out later today are similar to the past two
monthly readings—with core CPI inflation around 0.3 percent
or even higher—then a stronger policy move may be needed".
Regional Fed Presidents Goolsbee, Logan and Hammack also
kept the focus on inflation risks and stated or implied the FOMC

needs to act. Market participants have responded to this week’s
updates, pricing a 25bp hike in for the December and January
meetings, and a cumulative 3.5 hikes by September. We believe
this pricing overstates the need for policy tightening given core
inflation ex shelter remains relatively benign at 2.4% on a six-
month annualised basis and the labour market is balanced; but
it will take time for price concerns to subside.
While we agree that the US economy is strong in aggregate,

US households are under pressure from the cost of living, and
their investment decision making is constrained by borrowing
costs. Highlighting this, US existing home sales fell 5.5% in
October after a downwardly revised 1.0% gain in September,
leaving the level of sales below the cycle lows of 2009 and
2010. Our November Market Outlook provide a full update on
our expectations for the US and the world.
In Australia, the Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment Index

fell 2.7% to 78.2 in November, its weakest reading since
late-2022 and a level historically associated with periods of
severe economic stress. Renewed cost-of-living and interest
rate pressures were front and centre following another lift
in inflation, including sharply higher petrol prices, and the
RBA’s November rate increase. Mortgage holders are feeling
the squeeze most acutely, with sentiment across this group
falling to a record low. Consumers have become noticeably

more downbeat on the near-term economic outlook – the
relevant sub-index dropping 10.2% to be 22% below its
long-run average. Job-loss fears also continue to edge up.
Businesses are similarly troubled. The latest NAB business
survey showed confidence slipped further to –15 in October as
conditions held near their weakest level since the pandemic.
Of concern for the outlook, forward orders fell to –12, also
the weakest read since the pandemic, and capital expenditure

intentions deteriorated. Purchase costs continue to grow
materially faster than selling prices, further compressing
already tight margins.
Housing provides a clear example of how caution and tighter
financial conditions can jolt activity. The value of new dwelling
finance approvals plunged 15% in Q3, the largest quarterly
decline in the series’ history, taking approvals 25% lower year-
to-date. Investor lending bore the brunt of the adjustment,
falling 30% over the quarter as higher rates combined

with the housing tax changes announced in May. Owner-
occupier lending also weakened, however, with first-home
buyers proving particularly sensitive to the deterioration in
affordability and market conditions.
Taken together, the data points to a further loss of momentum
across the domestic economy, consistent with Westpac’s
expectation for subdued growth and concern over downside
activity risks.
Meanwhile, in the US, October’s headline CPI met our

expectations but at 0.4% was a touch above the consensus
view. Energy and food both contributed meaningfully to the
result, but core inflation was also marginally stronger than
forecast, +0.3%. Core goods inflation has accelerated over the
past four months to average a gain of 0.2% as businesses look
to pass tariffs, tech and energy input cost inflation through.
Shelter meanwhile continued to rise at an above-average pace,
gaining 0.3% in October.

The market impact of the October CPI report was accentuated
by hawkish remarks from Fed Governor Waller prior to the
release. He expressed confidence in economic activity and
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Post election, where are we left?
This week markets and the economy needed to digest
the General Election results, as well as data on how the
economy is tracking through the final months of the year. A
centre-right coalition looks more likely to ultimately prevail,

although it may be a while before we know for sure and
longer again before a stable government emerges. Watch
this space as paths to victory for the centre-left remain.
Economic data are consistent with a still-strong external
sector but a weaker domestically-generated growth
impulse. Our upcoming Economic Overview next week will
examine these trends in more depth.
The real star of the show this week was the outcome of the
2026 General Election, which delivered an election night lead

to the centre-right coalition of the National, ACT and NZ First
parties. The preliminary result was a 67-57 seat margin in an
enlarged 131 seat parliament. Hence the centre-right won on
election night with a slender one-seat margin, given that 66
seats are required to form a majority. Financial markets and
the business sector generally cheered this outcome given their
preference for a centre-right government. However, the margins
are tight and there is the potential for the result to switch around

when the final vote count becomes available on November 27.
National leads Labour by narrow margins in two electorates
(Wairarapa and Hamilton East) and could lose those seats
when all is said and done. Also, special votes tend to swing
more to the left than the right historically and hence could
boost the centre-left bloc. The star performer of the election
was the Opportunity Party which debuted in parliament with
7 seats. The centre-left would require their support to get

over the line in all scenarios, whereas the centre-right might or
might not need them depending on how the special votes and
final electorate results play out. There seems a clearer path to
victory for the centre-right – especially if they can come to an
agreement with Opportunity – but clients should be wary of
complacency (which we note may be a risk).
A further complication is that the election left the incumbent
National Party in a much-weakened position as their coalition

partners picked up seats at their expense, and because their
much-reduced share of the party vote (29.2%) meant that
no National Party list MPs made it into parliament. Several
seasoned and senior government ministers needed a list seat
to make it into the new government including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. Even if the final results confirm a centre-right
coalition win, there will be fierce debate within the coalition
on which party staffs key ministerial roles. ACT party leader

David Seymour is making a strong play for Finance Minister,
which Prime Minister Christopher Luxon is fiercely resisting.
There is the potential for tough and protracted coalition
negotiations to play out well into the Christmas period.
Indeed, I spoke on a post-election panel with an ex-National
Party senior minister this week who expressed the view that
it might not be until Waitangi Day (February 6) before a new
government is formed. Should anything like this play out then

the implications for economic and market uncertainty could
be significant. The initial relief rally in the New Zealand dollar
might prove premature in that scenario.
For interest rate markets, the main conclusion drawn is that as
a centre-left government seems a lower probability prospect,
then so also is the likelihood of a return to a ‘dual mandate’
for the RBNZ. Chief Economist Paul Conway made remarks
this week suggesting ongoing concern with inflation risks,

suggesting he is still in the hike camp at this stage. This has
seen markets firm up short-term OCR expectations such that a
25bp OCR increase in December is now seen as high probability
(85-90% currently). Bond markets cheered the prospect of
potentially tighter fiscal policy that could come with a centre-
right government. The spread between the 10-year government
bond and swap spread narrowed 4 basis points over the week
and the 10-year government bond yield differential to the US

fell to -49 basis points – the most negative since 2019.
Economic data showed further evidence of the two-speed
economy that is showing above-trend growth yet at the same
time feels quite unsatisfying to most voters. The low exchange
rate, generally robust global demand and tight supply
conditions in soft commodity markets have been delivering
consistently strong export returns for a couple of years now.
This week we saw another very strong GDT Pulse auction that

propelled whole milk powder futures higher – up some 20-25%
on year ago levels in USD terms. Significant upside risk for our
2026/27 Farmgate Milk Price forecast of $9.75/kgMS now exists,
which we will be delving into more deeply in our Economic
Overview to be released on Wednesday. The manufacturing
sector appears to remain in a solid state, with the manufacturing
PMI slipping a touch in October to 54.9 from 56.3 in September.
Short-term visitor arrivals have resumed their prior strong

uptrend, growing almost 3% for a second consecutive month
in September and almost 10% higher than a year earlier.
While arrivals from key European markets remain well below
year-earlier levels – likely reflecting the disruption to travel
through key Middle Eastern hubs along with higher jet fuel
prices – this has been more than offset by a very sharp lift in
arrivals from China. Indeed, Chinese arrivals in September
were almost two-thirds higher than the same month last year.

Michael Gordon
Senior Economist

### Page 5

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
And in the September quarter, arrivals from China surpassed
their pre-pandemic level for the first time. The number of
Australian citizens visiting New Zealand grew just under 6%yr
in September, but growth momentum from New Zealand’s
number one source market (a 30% share) does appear to have

slowed somewhat in recent months.
Less positive trends were evident in the more domestically
orientated areas of the economy. The most concrete indicator
of the NZ economy – concrete poured – dropped back a
touch in the September quarter, while consumer spending
in fell 0.7% in October. We have concerns on the quality
of the consumer spending data given that bank-sourced
data indicates a rise in consumer spending in October. But
nonetheless it’s probably reasonable to infer that higher fuel

costs in October crimped household budgets.
Housing market data for October were also week. House sales
fell and are down almost 22% y/y, while house prices fell in
October for the fifth consecutive month to be down almost
2% y/y. The weak performance of the housing market is likely
another factor weighing on consumer spending and residential
investment. That’s another topic we will be taking a look at in
next week’s Economic Overview.
Net migration flows for September confirm still modest

population growth. Arrivals of non-New Zealand citizens
continues to drift higher while departures declined a touch.
The net outflow of New Zealanders remains relatively high but
is declining, as signs of firmer employment growth emerge in
New Zealand at the same time as the Australian labour market
shows signs of weakening.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Oct Labour Force – Employment Change (000s)
Nov 19, Last: –39.3, Westpac f/c: 30
Market f/c: 27.5, Range: 9.9 to 42
Employment fell by 39.3k in September, well below expectations
for a 20k lift. Some of this weakness may reflect an unwind of
a possible residual seasonality stemming from recent changes

to the survey design, which saw August come in stronger.
Under the monthly noise, the labour market is loosening
gradually as jobs growth runs slower than population growth.
While some leading indicators point to near-term stability
– such as job ads – business surveys and detail within the
employment data point to some downside risk.
For October, we have pencilled in a lift in employment of +30k,
which would see employment growth hold steady on a three-

month average basis.
AUS: Nov Westpac Housing Sentiment (index)
Nov 17, Last: 85.3
The Westpac Housing Sentiment Index that appears in our
regular Housing Pulse reports will now be published on a
monthly basis a week after Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Sentiment is released. The Index is a composite
based on housing-related sentiment measures from the survey
and has proven to be an extremely useful predictor of turning
points in housing markets, both nationally and across states.

At 85.3, the Oct monthly Index read was holding near historic
lows, well below the long run avg of 100. The Nov update will
capture reactions to the RBA’s latest interest rate rise and the
deepening and broadening housing downturn. The release
will also include results from two new questions added to
the regular survey on consumers’ property buying and selling
intentions over the next twelve months.
AUS: Q3 Wage Price Index (%qtr)
Nov 18, Last: 0.8, Westpac f/c: 1.0

Market f/c: 1.0, Range: 0.9 to 1.1
The Wage Price Index (WPI) rose 0.8% in Q2, leaving annual
growth at 3.2%. Private sector wages continued to decelerate,
in line with a softer labour market, while public sector wages
provided a bit of an offset.
The Fair Work Commission's (FWC) 2026 Annual Wage Review
handed down a larger-than-expected 4.75% increase in award
wages. This will have a noticeable impact on the Q3 WPI, as the
wage increases stemming from the review are typically passed

on during the September quarter.
We have pencilled in a rise of 1.0% for Q3, lifting the annual
pace to 3.4%yr. Detail on wages growth by bargaining stream
will be closely watched for evidence of broader diffusion
beyond arrangements directly linked to the FWC's decision.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Oct Labour Force – Unemployment Rate (%)
Nov 19, Last: 4.7, Westpac f/c: 4.7
Market f/c: 4.7, Range: 4.6 to 4.8
Once again, the surprise in employment coincided with a surprise
in labour force participation, which fell 0.2ppts to 66.8% from
its near cycle high of 67.0% in August. This translated to a 26.2k

decline in the size of the labour force, which was less than the
drop in employment. As a result, the unemployment rate ticked
slightly higher in September, from 4.65% to 4.72% (although it
was steady at 4.7% to one decimal place), a new cycle high.
For October, we have the participation rate steady at 66.8%,
which together with the forecast rise in employment, would
see the unemployment rate also hold steady at 4.7%. Early
signs of rising retrenchments are a key signal to watch over the

coming months, especially if new hiring across other areas of
the economy starts to dry up.
NZ: Oct Selected Consumer Prices
Nov 17
Stats NZ’s monthly Selected Prices provides an update on
around half of the full CPI, including many of the more volatile
items. In terms of the big groups, we’re expecting a 0.1%
rise in overall food prices, with fresh produce prices typically
soft at this time of year. We also expect continued softness
in housing rents. However, the key focus will be on transport

costs. Petrol prices are expected to be up 11%. And we’ll be
watching to see if high global fuel prices are contributing to
further increases in airfares after recent large increases. There
is related uncertainty around holiday accommodation costs
(which drive much of the month-to-month volatility in overall
prices).
NZ: Q4 RBNZ Survey of Expectations (%yr)
Nov 17, Inflation 2yrs Ahead, Last: 2.34
With an easing in headline inflation in the September

quarter, we’re likely to see some easing in near-term inflation
expectations (one year ahead). However, longer term inflation
expectations, particularly the closely-watched two years
ahead measure, are likely to remain broadly steady. While
interest rates are moving higher, businesses are continuing to
report pressures on operating costs. In addition, despite their
recent easing, oil prices remain elevated. The only time longer-
term inflation expectations have moved materially below 2.3%

was in the mid-2010s. That followed an extended period when
inflation was running below 2%.

## Document 309: Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Central banks_ a pause while waiting for more information (2026-10-29).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

Central banks: a pause while waiting for more information

As expected, the Fed, the ECB, and the BoJ left their rates unchanged this week. This pause, however, does not mark 
the end of the cycle. The ECB’s tone is becoming more balanced, weighing inflationary risks against tightening financial 
conditions, but one final rate hike in December remains our base case. Across the Atlantic, Kevin Warsh describes an 
environment conducive to further tightening, while remaining cautious about the risk of a credit shock. In Japan, the 
lasting exit from the deflationary cycle is taking shape, and monetary policy adjustment is expected to continue 
gradually. We anticipate two rate hikes by the Fed by January and four by the BoJ by the second half of 2028.

Eurozone: A more balanced tone, but the course remains unchanged. The ECB’s statement marks a shift from September. 
It now explicitly acknowledges both upside risks to inflation (the magnitude and persistence of the energy shock) and downside 
pressures (tightening financial conditions). The statement that inflation would remain “well above target for an extended period” 
no longer appears. In essence, however, there is little change. A rate hike in December remains our base case scenario, widely 
anticipated and implicitly signaled by Christine Lagarde during the Q&A (“If the current situation is not called into question by the 
next set of economic data, the path is clear”). Lagarde presented the rise in sovereign yields as a global phenomenon that is 
tightening financial conditions; regarding the TPI, she stuck to the usual stance: the ECB will intervene if monetary policy 
transmission were to be hampered “by disorderly movements not justified by fundamentals”. By December, the indirect and 
second-round effects of the energy shock will be closely scrutinised. From our perspective, September’s producer prices offer 
clearer signals, but the increase remains limited to a few sectors: energy, electricity-intensive industries and electrical equipment, 
driven by IA1. For now, companies in the Eurozone, particularly in the services sector, appear to be absorbing the shock. The 
European Commission’s survey released this Thursday indicates a rise in the sales price index in October, which remains in line 
with recent months.

United States: The Fed navigates between risks. The FOMC pauses after September’s rate hike and maintains the federal 
funds target at 3.75%-4.0%. However, Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh outlined a landscape conducive to further tightening: stronger 
growth, “rather positive” labor market conditions, and, above all, inflation that is “too high.” As a result, two dissenting votes (out 
of 12) were cast in favor of a 25bps hike. But the committee also appears to fear the adverse consequences of tightening too 
quickly, which is why it is waiting to “accumulate more information.” AI is increasingly at the heart of monetary policymaking. In 
particular, K. Warsh warned against the risks of an abrupt tightening of interest rate conditions, including for the AI investment 
cycle - which triggered a steepening of the yield curve. He also announced that he would soon (on November 16) attend a hearing 
of the “Productivity and Employment” task force to “refine his views” on the implications of AI for the dual mandate. Governor Lisa 
Cook, who voted for a 25bps hike, had noted ahead of the committee meeting “ongoing inflationary pressures” and a risk that they 
might not “subside quickly”. In addition, K. Warsh announced that there would be fewer meetings resulting in rate decisions: 7 in 
2027 and 6 in 2028, compared with 8 today. The change is expected to take effect starting in the fall of 2027. The meetings freed 
up by this change would allow the committee to focus on “strategic deliberations” and long-term issues.

Japan: A status quo, but a tightening cycle set to continue as the economy emerges from deflation. The Bank of Japan 
decided, in line with our forecasts and the consensus, to keep its policy rate target steady at 1.25% at its October 29-30 meeting. 
One dissenting vote called for a rate hike (25bps), contrasting with the two dovish dissenting votes (opposed to a rate hike) at the 
previous meeting. As in the US, the decision is justified by the need to further analyse risks to economic activity, prices, and 
financial conditions. Above all, the central bank’s outlook for economic activity and prices marks a turning point: it explicitly states 
that “core inflation has reached a level generally in line with price stability” and that it is “expected to remain there.” Governor Ueda 
refined this by noting that it is now necessary to “anchor” inflation at this level before declaring the target achieved. Furthermore, 
the emphasis on “upside risks to prices” and the fact that any rise in core inflation is now deemed “undesirable” indicate that 
monetary tightening will continue.

The monetary tightening cycle continues. The next steps are likely to be more significant in the United States, where inflation 
has exceeded the target for nearly six years and coexists with a favorable outlook for growth and employment. In the Eurozone, 
the rise in inflation is primarily due to a supply shock in energy costs (though it is also fueled by demand in the United States). It 
continues to have little impact on measures of core inflation, including those tracked by the ECB, such as the HICP; and 
households’ long-term inflation expectations remain anchored. The degree of monetary tightening also differs. In real terms, the 
ECB’s current rates (the deposit facility rate deflated by the core CPI) hover around 0%, a level that more or less corresponds to 
estimates of the natural rate. The Fed, meanwhile, remains in accommodative territory: the real rate (Fed Funds deflated by Core

1 This is a point underscored by the first PPI releases for September (Spain, Italy).

### Page 2

PCE) is at around 0.8%, 40bps below the committee’s implied long-term level (1.2%). Japan remains an outlier: the rise in rates 
preceded the inflationary shock and, until now, has been more a matter of normalization. It is therefore more extensive and spread 
out over time. The central bank is adjusting to the new inflation regime and remains cautious in its exit from accommodative 
policies. The starting point is significantly lower, with real rates still negative, but the adjustment cycle should ultimately bring it to 
the upper end of the neutral range estimated by the BoJ (1.1%-2.5%). We therefore anticipate one final rate hike by the ECB in 
December, two more hikes by the Fed, first in December and then in January, and four spaced-out hikes by the BoJ through the 
second half of 2028.

Anis Bensaidani   
Economist, United States, Japan 
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

### Page 4

2027 ANNUAL OUTLOOK
As we approach 2027, we are faced with two competing trends. Capital, as defined by
interest rates, is delivering higher base returns, so our expectations for required returns to
invest have risen. At the same time the need for this capital continues to grow to deliver the
opportunities, and support the requirements, on the horizon. Companies and governments
are looking to investors to fund the opportunities and priorities ahead, at a time when capital

is more expensive and investment decisions must clear a higher bar.
In last year’s Outlook, we explored how three forces, artificial intelligence (AI), global
fragmentation and inflation, were defining a new market frontier. In 2027, that frontier must
be funded.
Companies need capital to realize the full potential of AI. Governments need capital to
strengthen security and resilience in a fragmented world. At the same time, inflation,
economic growth and central bank policy continue to shape the investment environment.

With cash earning a credible return, investors can afford to be discerning about where they
put capital to work.
That is especially true of the AI build-out. Markets are beginning to question whether the
scale of investment will ultimately pay off. We believe AI is a transformative technology
that will fundamentally change how we live and work. We also believe investors need to be
selective, with a focus on the companies that will define this new frontier.

In this environment, portfolio diversification remains critical. Forward-looking returns for
stock-bond portfolios are the most attractive in years, but market volatility creates a wide
range of possible outcomes. A diversified portfolio and a long-term perspective can help
investors navigate both.
The new market frontier is starting to take shape. As investors consider the opportunities
ahead, we are proud to serve as your financial partner and remain committed to helping you

achieve your long-term goals.
Foreword
David Frame
CEO,  J.P. Morgan Global Private Bank

What we got Assessment
Three forces are reshaping the investment
landscape: artificial intelligence (AI), global
fragmentation and inflation.
Scorecard for the 2026 Outlook

### Page 6

6
2027 ANNUAL OUTLOOK
In last year’s Outlook, we highlighted their Promise and Pressure,
and since then the salience of these themes has only grown.
AI offered the potential for higher corporate profits and faster
productivity growth, but also the risk of overinvestment,
exuberance and disruption. Fragmentation favored resilience and
security, but at the expense of cost, efficiency and government
budgets. Inflation had retreated from its post-pandemic peak but

seemed structurally higher and more vulnerable to rolling shocks.
Through 2026, markets moved to reflect the world we envisioned,
in some cases more emphatically than we expected.
Demand for AI continued to far outstrip the supply of AI
computing power (“compute” in the tech sector’s shorthand).
This has rewarded the companies designing, producing and
providing the semiconductors, components and power required
to deploy AI at scale. AI model providers generated real revenue

as businesses began paying up for AI-driven productivity gains.
Companies viewed as vulnerable to disruption underperformed.
Conflicts in Iran and Ukraine continued to highlight deep
vulnerabilities in energy supply chains and defense inventories.
The companies that we believe strengthen economic and national
security resilience outperformed even as traditional defense
companies lagged. Inflation remained above central bank targets,
bond yields climbed to multi-decade highs and a global rate-hiking

cycle kicked into gear.
Against that backdrop, a traditional balanced portfolio seems
set to deliver high single-digit returns. Real assets (such as
infrastructure and commodities) and active strategies (such
as diversified hedge funds) have earned their keep as portfolio
diversifiers.
The three forces (AI, fragmentation, inflation) continue to define
the economic and market environment. And they’re interacting
in sometimes unexpected ways that investors need to understand.

One theme links the three: An enormous demand for capital in both
the private and public sectors is raising the cost of capital. (It’s the
law of supply and demand we remember from Economics 101.)
Investors like you set the price for that capital. That is a position
of strength. It also compels decisions that cannot be deferred.
As many investors look to protect—as well as expand upon—several
years of handsome equity market gains, those decisions become

even more important.
Technology companies need your capital—trillions of dollars’
worth—to fund the next phase of the AI build-out. Governments
need it to finance security, resilience and rearmament initiatives.
Consumers, companies and sovereigns all need it to adapt to an
inflationary, fragmented world.
But inflation, resilient economic growth and central bank policy
mean that cash is more attractive. Today, it earns a real return.
Every financing decision, from a hyperscaler’s data center to a

government’s defense budget to your own portfolio allocation,
now carries an opportunity cost that was obscured for most of
the last cycle.
The AI boom will continue only if investors like you are willing
to provide the financing to bridge the gap between infrastructure
spending and consistent revenue. You will share the cost of
security with governments through taxes, inflation or both, but
you can also decide to own the beneficiaries of that investment.

And a higher cost of capital means every one of these decisions
must now clear a higher bar.
The old investing playbook may not power your portfolio through
the current cycle, even if forward looking returns for traditional
stock-bond mixes are still attractive. In the post–global financial
crisis cycle, policymakers kept the cost of capital low to incentivize
risk taking and boost asset prices. Today, capital has a price. We
believe there is a wider range of possible outcomes and, in the

context of public markets that have rarely been this concentrated,
an increased need to consider exposures you may not have.
Capital is the bridge to a world that is more secure and
productive. Your capital sets the price of progress. Place it wisely.

INFLATION, CENTRAL BANKS AND THE COST OF CAPITAL11
In 2027, central banks will matter again. Before you place your capital, you must make a
judgment about how far central bank policy rates will rise. Inflation is key to that decision
for central banks—and for your portfolio.
We believe that the Federal Reserve and other global central banks are raising rates to
adjust their policy stance and influence inflation expectations. They are not looking to

lower demand. If we are right, the expansion can continue, and our constructive view
on multi-asset portfolios will hold. If we are wrong, the cycle could end with a hiking
campaign that breaks economic demand and pressures risk assets.
While U.S. inflation has remained above the Fed’s 2% target, that reflects supply-driven
factors such as energy prices. The labor market is stable and wage growth is decelerating.
Thus, we believe that inflation will begin to moderate. We see no sign of a nascent

wage-price spiral or overheating economy that the Fed would need to correct with an
aggressive rate hiking campaign.
The European Central Bank (ECB) seems to be running a similar playbook, signaling it will
continue hiking only if it sees further effects of higher energy prices. On the other hand,
the People’s Bank of China (PBoC), facing persistent disinflation, seems likely to keep
policy accommodative for the foreseeable future.
Last year, we argued that we are in a new

regime where transformative technologies
and a changing geopolitical environment
drive investment returns more than
traditional forces such as GDP growth
or central bank policies.

### Page 13

INFLATION, CENTRAL BANKS AND THE COST OF CAPITAL
13
Inflation containment: Cost of
capital, not the policy rate, is doing
the heavy lifting
In the post–global financial crisis period, unprecedented monetary
policy (zero and negative interest rates and quantitative easing)
coupled with robust forward guidance from the Fed anchored
sovereign rates and compressed credit spreads and equity risk
premiums. In other words, capital had an “artificial” price. Today,

after substantial interest rate hikes, quantitative tightening and
the discontinuation of forward guidance, the price of capital is
more real.
Investors have as much to do with that change as central bankers.
Even before September, when the Fed raised its policy rate 25
basis points (bps), yields were already on the rise. Year to date the
10-year U.S. Treasury has risen roughly 75bps to around 5%. Fifty
of those basis points arrived in September alone, placing the move

in the top decile of monthly increases since 1962. Credit spreads
have widened modestly, investment grade (IG) by around 10bps,
high yield by roughly 40bps.
Importantly, markets are expecting more than two additional
hikes in the current cycle (the next one in December). That means
a higher cost of capital already constrains growth and inflation,
even absent an aggressive Fed.
What are the risks to our view?
What might compel the Fed to kick off an aggressive hiking cycle?

A stronger-than-expected economy, with wages accelerating
ahead of productivity, could force the Fed’s hand. So too could a
sustained move in 5-year/5-year inflation breakevens above 3%.
Indeed, the trajectory of data center construction is important
for bond yields. Ten-year U.S. Treasury yields peaked as investors
began to question further AI capex in 2027. A reacceleration in AI
infrastructure investment could push yields higher.
If higher diesel prices depress economic demand, it would present

a particular challenge to policymakers. In the United States, the
cost of diesel has risen to nearly $6.30/gallon from ~$3.80 before
the Middle East conflict began. Diesel at $10/gallon for a quarter
could make a meaningful dent in economic activity. The Fed would
then have to choose between hiking into a weakening economy
or risking that inflation expectations move higher. This is just one
example of the potential that the next growth downturn could be

accompanied by stubborn inflation, which would pose a challenge
for more traditional portfolios.

INFLATION, CENTRAL BANKS AND THE COST OF CAPITAL
Yes, inflation is still a threat
to your capital
Inflation is the foundation for our economic outlook, and we
believe it should also be reflected in the foundation of your
investment plan. Even though we expect inflation to moderate
through 2027, it still poses a key risk to the purchasing power
of your capital.
Many of our clients may also need to use a different benchmark
than policymakers and economists. From 2019 through 2025, the

headline PCE index rose at a ~3.5% annual rate. Over the same
period, based on our own clients’ spending data, average ticket
sizes on luxury goods purchases increased at more than a 6%
rate. Maintaining purchasing power for the goods and services
that matter most to you likely requires a higher hurdle than the
headline inflation index would suggest.

### Page 15
